    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

4. **ROADMAP**: These are D111 Action Matrix experiments - MF-3 requirement for C2 anchor variance estimation. 

The EMNLP ARR deadline is in 4 days, and both experiments are hitting GPU memory exhaustion—seed123 needs 592 MiB but only 529 MiB is available, while seed456 is trying to allocate 4.64 GiB with just 225 MiB free. The GPU is nearly maxed out in both cases, so I need to either free up memory or reduce the batch size. Both processes seem to have run on GPU 0 despite being assigned to different devices, or the memory accounting is showing logical rather than physical GPU assignments. The real issue is that seed123 had about 48 GiB of unaccounted memory usage alongside its 46 GiB process footprint, suggesting either concurrent processes or a peak memory spike during training that triggered the OOM.

Now I need to load the failed experiment's registry entry and the on-experiment-failed skill, then spawn a worker to diagnose the logs, update the registry, and report back to the Director.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have clear picture:

Both experiments failed with CUDA OOM on westd-11235:
- **seed123** (cuda:0): Process using 46.43 GiB, tried to allocate 592 MiB, only 529 MiB free. Total GPU: 94.97 GiB. So ~48 GiB is used by other processes on that GPU.
- **seed456** (cuda:1): Process using 94.74 GiB, tried to allocate 4.64 GiB, only 225 MiB free. This one consumed almost all memory.


- The discrepancy is odd though—if seed123 is only using 46.43 GiB on a 94.97 GiB GPU with 529 MiB free, that leaves ~48 GiB unaccounted for, which suggests either another process was running on the same GPU or CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES wasn't properly isolated between the two jobs.

Looking at the timing, both seed123 and seed456 failed after only about 2.5-2.7 minutes, which is much faster than the original seed42 experiment that completed successfully—so something went wrong early in these runs.

The memory numbers are telling: seed456 consumed nearly the entire GPU (94.74 GiB allocated out of 94.97 GiB available), which makes sense for full_trace with Qwen-7B. But seed123 only used 46.43 GiB directly, yet the GPU showed ~48 GiB unaccounted for—suggesting both processes were indeed running on the same physical GPU simultaneously, with seed456's memory appearing as "other" from seed123's perspective.

Since the original full_trace_seed42_v5 succeeded on a single GPU, this isn't a fundamental memory limitation with the model itself. The issue is likely that both experiments somehow got scheduled to the same GPU, or there's a batch size or code change that's causing problems. Looking at the GPU memory—94.97 GiB suggests a high-end GPU like an H100, though the exact model doesn't matter much. The real puzzle is why the parent experiment succeeded while these two crashed, and my leading hypothesis is that both processes somehow ended up on the same physical GPU despite the CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES assignment, causing an out-of-memory failure.

Other possibilities include competing processes consuming GPU memory, though the code itself hasn't changed between runs. I need to pull the worker logs to investigate what actually happened during those failed experiments.
```

{
  "task_type": "diagnose:qwen7b_full_trace_oom",
  "project": "trace-verdict-study",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断 qwen7b_full_trace_seed123 和 seed456 CUDA OOM 失败\n\n### 背景\n两个实验在 westd-11235 上同时跑（seed123 在 cuda:0，seed456 在 cuda:1），都因 CUDA OOM 失败：\n- seed123: 进程占 46.43 GiB，GPU 94.97 GiB 总量只剩 529 MiB（说明 ~48 GiB 被其他进程占了）\n- seed456: 进程占 94.74 GiB（PyTorch 分配 87.18 GiB），只剩 225 MiB\n\n这两个实验的 parent 是 full_trace_seed42_v5（已成功），同一代码 commit (e404237)，只是 seed 不同。\n\n### 你要做的\n\n1. **读日志**（用 ssh_read_only）：\n   - `cat ~/runs/trace-verdict-study/qwen7b_full_trace_seed123.log | tail -100`\n   - `cat ~/runs/trace-verdict-study/qwen7b_full_trace_seed456.log | tail -100`\n\n2. **检查 GPU 当前状态**：\n   - `nvidia-smi` 看当前 GPU 使用（是否有残留进程）\n   - `ps aux | grep -E 'train_sft|python' | grep -v grep` 看是否有残留训练进程\n\n3. **检查 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 设置**：\n   - 查看 tmux session 列表：`tmux ls`\n   - 检查 submit_training_job 设置的环境变量是否正确隔离了 GPU\n\n4. **检查 parent 实验的配置**：\n   - 查看 full_trace_seed42_v5 成功时用的参数：`cat ~/runs/trace-verdict-study/full_trace_seed42_v5.log | head -50`（看训练开始时的配置打印）\n\n5. **核心诊断问题**：\n   - 两个进程是否意外跑到了同一张物理 GPU？（seed123 只占 46 GiB 但 GPU 满了，暗示另一个进程在同一 GPU 上）\n   - full_trace condition 的序列长度是否导致 batch 内存峰值超过单 GPU 容量？\n   - 是否需要降低 batch_size 或启用 gradient checkpointing？\n\n6. **查看训练脚本默认配置**：\n   - `cat /root/trace-verdict-study/train/train_sft.py | head -80`（看默认超参，特别是 batch_size, max_seq_length, gradient_checkpointing）\n\n### 产出\n写一个简短的诊断报告到项目文件，包含：\n- 失败根因（确定的或最可能的）\n- 修复建议（具体的参数调整）\n- 是否需要改代码\n\n用 save_project_file 保存到 `artifacts/diag_qwen7b_full_trace_oom.md`\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：westd-11235\n- 账户：root\n- Python 环境：conda base（source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base）\n- 项目路径：/root/trace-verdict-study\n",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "knowledge"
  ]
}

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01KCzkDi2QNTR6pcnnNhZTZ5`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50

Good - there are already 2 Workers running:
1. My diagnostic Worker: `worker_diagnose_qwen7b_full_trace_oom_20260521T073438`
2. Another Worker: `worker_ops_diagnose_and_resubmit_full_trace_multiseed_20260521T073512` - this looks like it was spawned by another process (maybe from the seed456 failure callback)

Both GPUs (cuda:0 and cuda:1) are allocated but idle now - the experiments failed so nothing is running.


The situation is critical: both full_trace seeds OOM'd, leaving 2 GPUs idle with the EMNLP deadline just 4 days away, and these runs are essential for validating the multi-seed variance claim in MF-3. I should report the preliminary findings to the Director now while waiting for the diagnostic Worker to complete its analysis, then resubmit once I understand what caused the failures.

Looking at the crash patterns, both experiments failed within minutes of starting—seed123 after 156 seconds and seed456 after 166 seconds—while running simultaneously on separate GPUs. The memory snapshot reveals the real problem: seed123's process consumed 46.43 GiB with only 529 MiB free on cuda:0, meaning roughly 48 GiB was already occupied by other processes, and seed456 maxed out at 94.74 GiB on cuda:1. This suggests either severe memory contention from concurrent processes or that the full_trace condition itself requires more memory than the parent experiment did 6 days ago, possibly due to code or data changes. So the "other" memory is likely another process on the same GPU, but the real issue is that seed456 alone consumed nearly the entire 94.97 GiB GPU with just one run. The parent experiment succeeded despite using the same commit, which suggests either different hardware, different parameters, or gradient checkpointing that isn't being used now. I should let the Worker finish its diagnosis and check if there's already another Worker spawned from the seed456 callback.
```

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
